# Nervous system: reference images

Free to use commercially; looked at, never shipped or traced. Made by tools/atlas_refs.py from docs/sciences/anatomy-references.json. Each picture's source: the Wikipedias whose article on the structure shows it, its Wikidata image, or its Commons category.

## amygdalohippocampal area
